4.5.10 HSE Program
1. Basic HSE Requirements
1. Contractor should establish an effective HSE system and implement it strictly.

2. Contractor must adhere to the standards and regulations of Health, Safe and Environment constituted by the local
government.

3. Contractor must strictly adhere to the procedures / standards and regulations of Health, Safety and Environment
promulgated by NIDC.

4. Contractor should comply with all applicable local government regulations including guidelines and recommended
practices and any other Iranian authorities having jurisdiction.

5. All personnel should respect the local culture, traditions and social values of the indigenous population of the Iran and
South Azadegan region.

6. Contractor shall hire qualified and experienced worker and familiar his personnel with hazards especially with H2S
and Mine and UXO (unexploded ordinance) according to related procedure.

7. Contractor shall conduct proper training about UXO (unexploded ordinance) and mine awareness to all personnel.

2. Well Control
All the drilling operation shall obey the requirement of Well Control Manual of NIDC and contractor’s well control manual;
Under no circumstances should the deviation from well control policy be permitted without prior approval through NIDC.
If the contractor’s well control manual or anywhere has conflict with Well Control Manual of NIDC，take Well Control
Manual of NIDC as the standard.
Recording Reference
All BOP tests, drills, function tests, any malfunctions, repair or maintenance to the mud system and well control
equipment shall be recorded in the IADC daily reports and shall be signed by both the Drilling Contractor's Tool Pusher
and Drilling Supervisor on the well site. They shall also be recorded in the Daily Drilling Report.
Flow Check Procedure
To avoid a kick situation, it is recommended that a flow check be conducted at specific times during the drilling operation.
1. While drilling (any warning sign).

2. Prior to tripping the drill string.

3. While tripping (any warning sign).

4. While out of hole (continuously).

5. Trip out: 
 After pulling 5% of the drill pipe 
 At the midpoint depth of the wellbore 
 Prior to pulling the first stand of drill collars 
 After all the drill string is out of hole.
6. Trip in: 
 Upon reaching surface casing shoe
                                       TADBIR DRILLING DEVELOPMENT COMPANY:     P | 127

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan Oil Field EPDS Project
                         Drilling & Completion of 15 Wells and Completion of 2 Work Over Wells

   Contract No.        Co.            Prj.           Dis.        Dep.        Doc.        Rev.         Well No.
  CCA/C/1400/1     TDDC      SAZN     PM      ENG     WDP        00      G21(SAZNXXX)

 At midpoint of the wellbore

Primary Control Reference
It is the responsibility of the Drilling Contractor to keep the hole full at all times by using all the available equipment. The
Drilling Supervisor shall monitor that the correct preventive practices are being implemented and/or conducted at all
times.
Extreme care shall be taken to monitor mud volume, drilling breaks and gas cut mud.
The MACP (Maximum allowable casing pressure) shall be confirmed by the Drilling Supervisor, either after setting each
new casing string or, whenever the density of the drilling mud changes.
The MACP shall be clearly written on a kick control sheet that will be posted near the choke control panel.
The predetermined distance at which the Tool Joint is to be pulled above the rotary should be posted on rig floor near
the BOP control panel.
The Driller is responsible for carefully measuring and recording the slow pump pressure. Awareness of these values is
an important element in killing operations, in order to avoid formation breakdown.
Take a measure and record the slow pump pressure at three different flow rates with all pumps at the following times: 
 Once per tour. 
 Whenever changes occur in the mud density/rheology. 

 Whenever changes occur in the dimension and characteristics of the string, BHA, jet size, jet plugged or jet lost, etc.

Secondary Control Reference
Any time a drilling break is noticed, drilling shall stop and a static monitoring of the well shall be carried out. Space-out
for all Pipe Rams and Annular Preventer must be known to the Driller at all times to keep tool joints clear of annular
preventer and rams.
Kick Detection Procedure While Drilling
If there is an indication of a possible kick occurring, the Driller shall follow the kick detection procedures outlined below:
Stop drilling with the pump onPick up top drive to a predetermined space-out positionShut off the pumps and check for
well flow by visual examination on the bell nipple or flow line.
1) If the well is not flowing: 
 Immediately notify the rig manager, Drilling Supervisor and Tool pusher. 

 The Drilling Supervisor will decide whether to circulate bottoms up or resume drilling. The penetration rate will be limited
as long as the cause of the abnormal situation is undetermined. Always be aware that some hydrocarbons (gas or oil
cut mud) may rise to the surface. Ensure that the proper monitoring and safety systems all in place.
2) If the well is flowing: 
 Close the well as per the Shut-in Procedure. 
 Immediately notify the Well site Drilling Supervisor, rig manager and Tool pusher.
3. Health
1. This well is committed to develop for oil in a responsible manner that promotes employees' and local inhabitants'
health.

2. A trained Paramedic and proper MEDIVAC equipment and ambulance shall be stationed on the drilling rig during the
drilling campaign.

3. An emergency medical evacuation plan shall be coordinated, and shall keep in touch with emergency response
centres manned by qualified doctor 24-hours per day in Ahwaz city.

4. Medical emergency report and investigation shall be performed as soon as possible in accordance with Medical
Emergency Procedures.

4. Safety
1. Contractor shall make detail operation procedures which have covered all related activities.

2. Due to high concentration of H2S in Sarvak, Kazhdumi and Gadvan reservoirs, make detail H2S operation procedures,
in particular, and carry it out strictly.

3. Contractor must provide lifesaving equipment like SCBA, Escape Mask, H2S detectors (Multi portable and personnel
detector) and implement the H2S Services system during drilling activity.

4. All lifting equipment and cranes must be certified by approved 3rd party (approved by Institute of Standards & Industrial
Research of Iran)

5. All electrical equipment should be designed for such locations, and listed by nationally recognized testing laboratory.
All wiring components and  electrical equipment should be maintained  in accordance with the manufacturers’
recommendation.

6. Contractor shall implement the risk assessment and Job Safety Analysis before starting the drilling activity.

7. PSSR (Pre-Startup Safety Review) before spud-in and penetrating into oil and gas formations are mandatory by
contractor under the supervision of Company and approved by Company.

8. Contractor shall pay much attention to fire, explosion prevention and well control.

9. Contractor shall make detail Emergency Response Plan and Evacuation Plan.

10. Contractor shall do efforts to keep employees’ safety as possible.

5. Environment
Requirement on location
1. The access road and well location construction shall be accorded with construction program of well location.  It
minimizes any impacts on the local residents, prevent all soil contamination, prevent all ground water contamination,
and prevent all surface water contamination. Cutting and waste water pit shall be lining and drainage system shall be
good and to avoid farm and season canal contamination.

2. Management regulation for transportation truck, drilling operation machine and tools shall be carried out strictly to
avoid damage farm and trees, etc.

3. Well site shall be flat and clean. All the equipment, materials, drilling tools shall be put in order.

4. Control the rig noise to decrease the influence to surroundings while drilling at the inhabitation area.

5. Contractor shall perform on site segregation of wastes prior to transfer to the transfer area and/or delivery to the
transportation Contractor in accordance with the following waste hazard classification.

Non-Hazardous Wastes (Re-usable, Recyclable and Recoverable Wastes)
1. Ferrous and non-ferrous metals, plastics, and wood wastes, PETs and potentially paper and glass

2. Materials shall be separately segregated and provision may be made for size reduction and containerization
equipment.

3. The Contractor/Generators are encouraged to seek for recycle/re-use alternatives based on the Local available
facilities, for final disposal of these materials.

4. It should be noted that landfill disposal of metal waste materials and wood wastes shall be prohibited. For wood
wastes re-use of and/or salvage of commercial timber, or deliver/release to Local community are recommended.
Hazardous Wastes
Hazardous Waste, such as clinic waste, oil waste, waste produced by painting, insulation works shall be duly segregated.
Facilities of environment protection supplied to well site
1. Mud purifying circulation system

2. Cooling water spraying and circulating system for drilling pump

3. Special recovery tank for deposed oil

4. Reserve house for valuable mud chemicals

5. Automatic mud weighting supply system

6. BOP stack is equipped according to drilling design

7. Define a separation area for gathering all waste according to waste management procedure

Requirement of environment protection on location
1. Sewage pit Area of the sewage pit: 1500 m2

2. Prior to spud mud pit and sewage pit must comply with the standard. Install cooling water spraying and circulating
system for drilling pump and sewage circulating system for cleaning rig floor and equipment.

3. Install BOP stack and pressure test shall be operated according to the drilling design to avoid blowout.

4. Install oil supply lines and check whether there is any leakage.

Management of environment protection on location
1. In drilling operation keep mud and sewage from overflowing. If it happens, clean up timely.

2. Mud chemicals shall be stored according to the standard and shall not be scattered around well site. If mud chemicals
scatter while loading or unloading, clean it up at once. The sack of mud chemicals shall be recovered. Do not dispose it
on site.

3. Enhance water management at well location and do not wash equipment with fresh water. Stop water flowing for long
time. Overhead tanks shall be utilized.

4. Oil management shall be carefully at the well site. No oil dirt on site, no dripping and leaking at oil tank and manifold
system area.

5. After the well is completed, reinforce the sewage pi solidify the disposed mud and have environment procedure
fulfilled.

6. Install fuel supply lines and keep them in good condition.

7. All the drilling fluid materials shall be recovered and shall not be left at the well site after completion.

8. The environmental protection personnel from contractor and operator side shall check and accept the well site
environment according to the regulation after completion. Only well site environment is qualified, the well can be
delivered.

9. Transition Area for Hazardous Waste shall also be provided by each Contractor in his allocated area,

10. Company shall approve each area before use as well. The transition area of hazardous waste shall. 
 Paved, fenced, marked and illuminated 

 Equipped with appropriate Fire Extinguisher and other emergency response equipment 

 (e.g. Eye-Wash and Emergency Shower, appropriate First Aid kits, neutralizer substances etc.). 
 In compliance with the requirements of handling, transportation and storage of hazardous waste (based on usage
chemical MSDS). 
 Configured to accommodate a safe physical separation of incompatible waste materials (e.g., acids, and alkalis, organic
materials and strong oxidizing agents). 

 Capable enough to provide storage for the entire range of hazardous wastes generated during the work. 
 The area shall be provided with Spill Containment and means of preventing run‐off to and from the area. 
  All waste containers to be stored shall be appropriately labeled. 
 Hazardous Waste shall be transferred in a timely manner, within a maximum period of 48 hours, for any period in
excess of this permission shall be granted from Company and/or PEDEC.

Standard of utilization and discharging of wastes on location
1. Conduct drilling operation according to the design, drilling mud is needed before spud.

2. Sewage that cannot be recovered shall be put into the pit do not discharge. If the volume of sewage is too big and
some should be discharged the quality of drainage must comply with the following regulations.

       Maximum permitted concentration of wastes in water

    Number   Items       Concentration   Number    Items                 Concentration(
                                                                                    mg/l)
    1       PH         6~9             4            Volatilization Powder   0.5
    2        Crude Oil   10              5          Sulphide             1
    3          Floating    100             6         Oxygen  Consumed  100
               Objects                              by Chemicals

3. Make full use of waste water. Water in mud pump and water brake must be utilized by circulating. Water that is used
to wash drill floor, shall be recovered after deposition. The recovery rate of waste water shall reach 85％.

4. Disposed oil shall be recovered entirely. Recovery rate shall be 100％. Dumping of waste oil shall be prohibited

5. In drilling operation, non-chrome thinner high temperature mud system shall be used often if engineering is qualified.

6. While drilling in special area adopt mud creation inhabitation techniques to decrease the waste mud creation.

7. After completion, all the mud chemicals, bentonite, clays shall be recovered and shall not be left at the well site. Mud
in purifying tank shall also be recovered after completion.

8. In case of any flammable or noxious gas encountered steps shall be taken at once to prevent kicks and blowout. Gas
shall be guided into ignition equipment to burn it away.

9. Burning material that may result in serious smog or horrible smell at the well site is not allowed. For cement or barite
that may result in granular dust pollution operation shall be performed in a closed system.

10. For abandoned well filled with cement plugs to seal the oil, gas or water formation.

11. Contractor/Generators are required to transport all generated wastes to the location/facilities designated, and
licensed by authorities and/or approved/verified by the Company/PEDEC (as appropriate).

12. All wastes shall be transferred by approved Sub‐Contractor (which could be a 3rd party Sub-Contractor of the
Contractor/Generator itself). The Waste Transporter and any other waste management service Contractor shall be
licensed in accordance with local regulations and are in compliance with such regulations. Company shall verify and
approve the Waste Transportation Contractor by checking licenses and qualifications, audit and inspections, verification
of human resources and received trainings etc.

13. The transportation Contractor shall ensure that all wastes are transported in a safe and responsible manner

  CCA/C/1400/1     TDDC      SAZN     PM      ENG     WDP        00      G21(SAZNXXX)

6. H2S monitoring and prevention measure
6.1. Monitoring H2S
1. Somewhere on the well site is apt to accumulate H2S, such as mud pit, shale shaker, and drilling platform, should be
installed one set of. Make sure H2S monitor and alarm system can be used.

2. Alarm threshold setting for H2S warning indicator: the first warning level is 10mg/m3, unable warning sound, just show
present value of H2S content to operators. The second warning level is 20mg/m3; when the H2S content in air exceed
20mg/m3, H2S warning indicator can give an alarm automatically, and warning sound should be heard by everyone
work in well site. The audible alarm should be installed on Kelly board.

3. Equip enough gas masks on drilling platform, shaker screen (total amount should large than 24), and equip 12 sets
of 30min positive pressure Air Aspirators，2 sets of portable H2S monitor and appropriative H2S warning indicator.
Arrange a person specially assigned for taking charge all the protective apparatus. This man must do periodical check
to keep these equipment in a good condition.

4. H2S monitor should be done weekly check and compulsory examination.

5. After drilling into the pay zone, strengthen the detection of H2S content in mud.

6.2. Measures for H2S prevention
1. Windsocks shall be set on the drill floor and in the monsoon direction or/and prevailing wind direction of Azadegan oil
field, off well site. In emergency, employees must follow the evacuation procedure.

2. Explosion-proof blowers shall be installed around the rig, shale shaker, and drilling fluid tanks to blow away toxic and
flammable gas at the well site.

3. The rig crew in the H2S area shall be furnished with H2S monitors and adequate protective apparatus. Everybody
shall be able to use, maintain, and check the apparatus.

4. The technicians of rig crew are responsible for H2S proof training and the tool pusher is in charge of the supervision
and examination

5. The pH value of drilling fluid in the H2S area shall be kept over 9.5. Pay attention to measurement of H2S concentration
in drilling fluid. The H2S concentration shall be kept at less than 50mg/cm3 by using sulphureted removal agents and
degassers.

6. Personnel safety and protection shall be carried out according to SY/T5087 during operations in polluted areas where
H2S content exceeds the critical concentration.

7. When the rig crew makes decision to bleed and fire because well control cannot be performed due to well site
conditions, operations shall be performed according to procedures in SY/T5087.

8. Once blowout occurs, the incident shall be reported to the higher-level department as soon as possible and firefighting
trucks, ambulances, medical care personnel and technicians shall be on duty at the well site.

9. After the blowout is controlled, H2S concentration should be measured in the places where H2S is possible to gather.

10. The personnel are not allowed to access until the H2S concentration is below the critical concentration

                                       TADBIR DRILLING DEVELOPMENT COMPANY:     P | 133
